// #gwh
if (user.quests.MiniEventSnowballShowdown) gwh();
function gwh() {
  const isProd = false;
  const targetEnvironmentType = 'greatWinterHunt';
  const enviromentType = getEnvironmentType();
  const todayUTC = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const gwhAreas = {
    hill: 'winter_hunt_grove',
    workshop: 'winter_hunt_workshop',
    fortress: 'winter_hunt_fortress'
  };
  const scrollChests = {
    'Naughty List': 'naughty_2022_list_chest_convertible'
  };
  if (
    [
      'winter_hunt_grove',
      'winter_hunt_workshop',
      'winter_hunt_fortress'
    ].indexOf(enviromentType) < 0
  )
    return;
  logging('gwh_1.2.20.2.15');
  // prettier-ignore
  // const upgradePlans = [
  //   [2, 1, 1], [2, 2, 1], [2, 2, 2],
  //   [3, 2, 2], [3, 3, 2], [3, 3, 3],
  //   [4, 3, 3], [4, 4, 3], [4, 4, 4],
  //   [5, 4, 4], [6, 4, 4], [6, 5, 4],
  //   [6, 6, 4], [6, 6, 5], [6, 6, 6],
  //   [7, 6, 6], [8, 6, 6], [8, 7, 6],
  //   [8, 8, 6], [8, 8, 7], [8, 8, 8],
  //   [9, 8, 8], [10, 8, 8], [10, 9, 8],
  //   [10, 10, 8], [10, 10, 9], [10, 10, 10],
  //   [11, 10, 10], [11, 11, 10], [11, 11, 11],
  //   [12, 11, 11], [12, 12, 11], [12, 12, 12],
  //   [13, 12, 12], [13, 13, 12], [13, 13, 13],
  //   [14, 13, 13], [14, 14, 13], [14, 14, 14],
  //   [15, 14, 14], [15, 15, 14], [15, 15, 15],
  //   [16, 15, 15], [16, 16, 15], [16, 16, 16],
  //   [17, 16, 16], [17, 17, 16], [17, 17, 17],
  //   [18, 17, 17], [18, 18, 17], [18, 18, 18],
  //   [19, 18, 18], [19, 19, 18], [19, 19, 19],
  //   [20, 19, 19], [20, 20, 19], [20, 20, 20]
  // ]
  const ppTrap = 'ppTrap';
  // const fsTrap = 'fsTrap';
  const normal = 'normal';
  const gotd = 'gotd';
  const fi = 'floating_islands';
  // const ka = 'kings_arms';
  // const qr = 'queso_river';
  // const qp = 'queso_plains';
  // const qq = 'queso_quarry';
  const pp = 'Pecan Pecorino';
  const gouda = 'Gouda Cheese';
  // const sb = 'SUPER';
  // const bestArcane = 'best.weapon.arcane';
  // const bestPhysical = 'best.weapon.physical';
  const bestTactical = 'best.weapon.tactical';
  const bct = 'Boiling Cauldron';
  const aeib = 'Attuned Enerchi Induction';
  const gotdB = 'Seasonal Gift';
  const gotdC = 'Tarnished Charm,Unstable Charm,Gift Wrapped,None';
  const enerchi = 'Enerchi Charm';
  // const lk2C = 'Spooky Charm,Snowball Charm,Cactus Charm,Luck Charm';
  const lk3C = 'Luck Charm';
  const st = {
    defaultTarget: [fi, fi, fi],
    // grove ppTrap
    gT: bct,
    gnB: aeib,
    gnC: enerchi,
    ggC: gotdC,
    // workshop ppTrap
    wn1: bestTactical,
    wnB: aeib,
    wnC: enerchi,
    wg1: bestTactical,
    wgC: gotdC,
    // fortress ppTrap
    fT: bestTactical,
    fnB: aeib,
    fnC: lk3C,
    fgC: lk3C,
    // fortress fsTrap
    fsnB: aeib,
    fsnC: lk3C,
    fsgC: lk3C,
    // normalTrap
    n0: gouda,
    nT: bct,
    nnB: aeib,
    nnC: enerchi,
    ngC: gotdC
  };
  const defaultSettings = {
    startPpQty: 14,
    ppKeptQty: 2,
    startCinnamonQty: 37,
    cinnamonKeptQty: 3,
    fsKeptQty: 3,
    goHailstoneQty: 27,
    hailstoneKeptQty: 2,
    isAutoGolem: true,
    isAutoUpgrade: true,
    maxGolemsUse: 3,
    golemSetup: { defaultTarget: st.defaultTarget },
    minGolemDelayTime: 3500,
    maxGolemDelayTime: 5000,
    isGoodGoTd: true,
    badGotdDates: [],
    isAutoMapping: true,
    isMapping: false,
    shutdown: {
      isLeaveFortressIf36: true,
      isForgeOffIf12: true
    },
    normalTrap: {
      normal: [st.n0, st.nT, st.nnB, st.nnC, false, [false, false, false]],
      gotd: [st.n0, st.nT, gotdB, st.ngC, false, [false, false, false]]
    },
    winter_hunt_grove: {
      ppTrap: {
        normal: [pp, st.gT, st.gnB, st.gnC, false, [false, false, false]],
        gotd: [pp, st.gT, gotdB, st.ggC, false, [false, false, false]]
      }
    },
    winter_hunt_workshop: {
      ppTrap: {
        normal: [pp, st.wn1, st.wnB, st.wnC, false, [false, false, false]],
        gotd: [pp, st.wg1, gotdB, st.wgC, false, [false, false, false]]
      }
    },
    winter_hunt_fortress: {
      ppTrap: {
        normal: [pp, st.fT, st.fnB, st.fnC, false, [false, false, false]],
        gotd: [pp, st.fT, gotdB, st.fgC, false, [false, false, false]]
      },
      fsTrap: {
        normal: [pp, st.fT, st.fsnB, st.fsnC, true, [true, false, false]],
        gotd: [pp, st.fT, gotdB, st.fsgC, true, [true, false, false]]
      }
    },
    areaBaits: {
      pp: {
        id: 2522,
        type: 'pecan_pecorino_cheese',
        name: 'Pecan Pecorino Cheese'
      },
      gpp: {
        id: 2733,
        type: 'glazed_pecan_pecorino_cheese',
        name: 'Glazed Pecan Pecorino Cheese'
      }
    }
  };
  const settings = isProd
    ? getStorageToObject(targetEnvironmentType, defaultSettings)
    : defaultSettings;
  let map = null;
  let mapInfo = null;
  const myItems = { pp: 0, gpp: 0, cinnamon: 0, hailstone: 0, fs: 0 }; // ;null;
  let isStartFortress = false;
  let isStartWorkshop = false;
  let isEndWorkshop = false;
  run();
  function run() {
    console.log('run');
    autoSettings();
  }
  function autoSettings() {
    console.log('autoSettings');
    if (settings.badGotdDates && settings.badGotdDates.indexOf(todayUTC) > -1)
      settings.isGoodGoTd = false;
    if (!settings.isAutoMapping) {
      checkArea(settings);
      return;
    }
    autoMapping(() => {
      map = getMapByClass(MapClasses.event);
      if (!map) {
        checkArea(settings);
        return;
      }
      hg.utils.TreasureMapUtil.getMapInfo(map.map_id, (data) => {
        const info = isProd ? data : mapInfo;
        mapInfo = parseMapInfo(info);
        console.log('mapInfo:', mapInfo);
        checkArea(settings);
      });
    });
  }
  function parseMapInfo(mapInfo) {
    const rtn = {};
    const hunters = mapInfo.treasure_map.hunters;
    let completed = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < hunters.length; i++) {
      const hunter = hunters[i];
      const completedIds = hunter.completed_goal_ids.mouse;
      if (completedIds && completedIds.length > 0)
        completed = completed.concat(completedIds);
    }
    console.log('completed mouse ids', completed);
    const mouses = mapInfo.treasure_map.goals.mouse;
    for (let i = 0; i < mouses.length; i++) {
      const mouse = mouses[i];
      if (completed.indexOf(mouse.unique_id) > -1) continue;
      const type = mouse.type;
      if (['joy', 'glazed'].indexOf(type) > -1) rtn.isGpp = true;
      const areaIds = mouse.environment_ids;
      if (areaIds && areaIds.length === 1) {
        let area = null;
        switch (areaIds[0]) {
          case 68:
            area = gwhAreas.fortress;
            break;
          case 69:
            area = gwhAreas.hill;
            break;
          case 70:
            area = gwhAreas.workshop;
            break;
          default:
            break;
        }
        if (!rtn[area]) rtn[area] = [];
        rtn[area].push(type);
      } else {
        if (!rtn['all']) rtn['all'] = [];
        rtn['all'].push(type);
      }
    }
    return rtn;
  }
  function autoMapping(callback) {
    console.log('autoMapping');
    // map completed
    map = getMapByClass(MapClasses.event);
    if (map && map.is_complete) {
      const mapName = map.name;
      console.log(`Claim map reward, id: ${map.map_id}, name: ${mapName}`);
      hg.utils.TreasureMapUtil.claim(map.map_id, () => {
        setTimeout(() => {
          hg.utils.UserInventory.useConvertible(
            scrollChests[mapName],
            1,
            (data) => {
              console.log(data.success == 1 ? 'success' : 'failed', data);
              map = null;
              setTimeout(() => {
                if (callback) setTimeout(callback, 2000);
              }, 2000);
            }
          );
        }, 1000);
      });
      return;
    }
    callback();
  }
  function checkArea(setup) {
    const enviromentType = getEnvironmentType();
    console.log('checkArea:', enviromentType, setup);
    switch (enviromentType) {
      case gwhAreas.hill:
        cinnamonHill(setup);
        break;
      case gwhAreas.workshop:
        golemWorkshop(setup);
        break;
      case gwhAreas.fortress:
        iceFortress(setup);
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
  }
  // Get item information from location-specific quest in location-specific algorithm.
  // check trap by item information and run location-specific algorithm.
  function iceFortress(setup) {
    console.log('iceFortress(', setup);
    const quest = isProd ? user.quests.QuestIceFortress : QuestIceFortress;
    const items = quest.items;
    initMyItems(items, setup);
    const isShutdown = quest.is_shutdown_enabled;
    if (isShutdown) {
      if (
        !setup.isMapping &&
        setup.shutdown.isLeaveFortressIf36 &&
        quest.shield.health == 36 &&
        myItems.hailstone < setup.goHailstoneQty
      ) {
        travel(gwhAreas.workshop, 2000, () => {
          checkTrap(setup);
        });
        return;
      }
    }
    if (isMapTravelling(setup)) return;
    // auto mapping時要兼顧破壁,所以有 map mice + 有 pp + 有 FS才留在 workshop
    // 這邊不強制中斷
    // checkTrap(setup);
    // return;
    let trap = null;
    console.log('Boss跟 Break shield都需要 FS,但 Boss不需要 hailstone跟 PP');
    if (myItems.fs > setup.fsKeptQty) {
      if (quest.shield.health == 0) {
        console.log('Frost King');
        trap = getNormalTrap(setup);
        trap[0] = 'SUPER';
        trap[2] = 'Seasonal Gift';
        trap[3] = 'none';
        trap[4] = true;
        trap[5] = [false, false, false];
        handleTrap(trap, quest, setup);
        return;
      }
      console.log('Breaking shield');
      if (
        myItems.pp > setup.ppKeptQty &&
        myItems.hailstone > setup.hailstoneKeptQty
      ) {
        trap =
          setup.winter_hunt_fortress.fsTrap[setup.isGoodGoTd ? gotd : normal];
        handleTrap(trap, quest, setup);
        return;
      }
    }
    // 即使強制 mapping, pp不夠也不留在 fortress. pp夠的話,用不破壁設定
    if (setup.isMapping) {
      if (myItems.pp > setup.ppKeptQty) {
        trap =
          setup.winter_hunt_fortress.ppTrap[setup.isGoodGoTd ? gotd : normal];
        handleTrap(trap, quest, setup);
        return;
      }
      travel(gwhAreas.hill, 2000, () => {
        checkArea(setup);
      });
      return;
    }
    // 到這邊就是沒 event cheese, FS或 hailstone,離開
    // 因為FS只在破壁/Boss時用.有 FS在 Fortess用 normal cheese是損失
    // 除了強制 mapping(強制 mapping時停用 FS)跟 boss外,不在 Fortess用 pp/gpp以外的 cheese
    travel(gwhAreas.workshop, 2000, () => {
      checkArea(setup);
    });
  }
  function isMapTravelling(setup) {
    console.log('isMapTravelling(', setup);
    const isLeaving = true;
    const isNotLeaving = false;
    if (!setup.isAutoMapping) return isNotLeaving;
    if (setup.isMapping) return isNotLeaving;
    // 這裡已經是 isAutoMapping了,沒 map/mapInfo一律先回 Hill
    // 可以觀察看看要一律先回 Hill還是視同 !setup.isAutoMapping
    if (!map || !mapInfo) {
      if (enviromentType === gwhAreas.hill) return isNotLeaving;
      travel(gwhAreas.hill, 2000, () => {
        checkArea(setup);
      });
      return isLeaving;
    }
    const enviromentType = getEnvironmentType();
    if (mapInfo[enviromentType]) return isNotLeaving;
    if (mapInfo[gwhAreas.fortress] && isStartFortress) {
      travel(gwhAreas.fortress, 2000, () => {
        checkArea(setup);
      });
      return isLeaving;
    }
    if (mapInfo[gwhAreas.workshop] && isStartWorkshop) {
      travel(gwhAreas.workshop, 2000, () => {
        checkArea(setup);
      });
      return isLeaving;
    }
    if (enviromentType === gwhAreas.hill) return isNotLeaving;
    travel(gwhAreas.hill, 2000, () => {
      checkArea(setup);
    });
    return isLeaving;
  }
  function initMyItems(items, setup) {
    console.log('initMyItems', items, setup);
    myItems.pp = MyUtils.parseQuantity(items.pecan_pecorino_cheese.quantity);
    myItems.gpp = MyUtils.parseQuantity(
      items.glazed_pecan_pecorino_cheese.quantity
    );
    myItems.cinnamon = MyUtils.parseQuantity(
      items.compressed_cinnamon_coal_stat_item.quantity
    );
    myItems.hailstone = MyUtils.parseQuantity(
      items.hailstone_stat_item.quantity
    );
    myItems.fs = MyUtils.parseQuantity(items.festive_spirit_stat_item.quantity);
    isStartFortress =
      myItems.pp > setup.startPpQty &&
      myItems.hailstone > setup.goHailstoneQty &&
      myItems.fs > setup.fsKeptQty;
    isStartWorkshop = myItems.cinnamon > setup.startCinnamonQty;
    isEndWorkshop = myItems.cinnamon < setup.cinnamonKeptQty;
  }
  function golemWorkshop(setup) {
    console.log('golemWorkshop(', setup);
    const quest = user.quests.QuestGolemWorkshop;
    const items = quest.items;
    initMyItems(items, setup);
    const isShutdown = quest.is_shutdown_enabled;
    if (isShutdown) {
      if (setup.shutdown.isForgeOffIf12) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          const quest = user.quests.QuestGolemWorkshop;
          // prettier-ignore
          const a = [
            quest.workshop.head.num_hunts_remaining != 12 && quest.workshop.head.is_active,
            quest.workshop.torso.num_hunts_remaining != 12 && quest.workshop.torso.is_active,
            quest.workshop.limb.num_hunts_remaining != 6 && quest.workshop.limb.is_active
          ];
          toggleForges(a);
        }, Math.floor(12000 + Math.random() * 3000));
      }
    }
    if (isMapTravelling(setup)) return;
    // auto mapping要兼顧 forge
    if (isEndWorkshop) {
      console.log('travel to Cinnamon Hill');
      travel(gwhAreas.hill, 2000, () => {
        checkArea(setup);
      });
    }
    checkTrap(setup);
    return;
  }
  function cinnamonHill(setup) {
    console.log('cinnamonHill(', setup);
    const quest = user.quests.QuestCinnamonTreeGrove;
    const items = quest.items;
    initMyItems(items, setup);
    if (isMapTravelling(setup)) return;
    // Hill是 mapping起點,特別處理
    if (setup.isAutoMapping) {
      if (!map) {
        handleTrap(getNormalTrap(setup), quest, setup);
        return;
      }
      // auto mapping時不 checkCinnamon
      checkTrap(setup);
      return;
    }
    if (checkCinnamon(setup)) return;
    checkTrap(setup);
  }
  function checkCinnamon(setup) {
    if (isStartWorkshop) {
      console.log('travel to Golem Workshop');
      travel(gwhAreas.workshop, 2000, () => {
        checkArea(setup);
      });
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function checkTrap(setup) {
    const enviromentType = getEnvironmentType();
    const quest = getQuest();
    let trap = null;
    if (myItems.pp > setup.startPpQty) {
      trap = setup[enviromentType][ppTrap][setup.isGoodGoTd ? gotd : normal];
    } else if (myItems.pp <= setup.ppKeptQty) {
      trap = getNormalTrap(setup);
    } else {
      const areaBaitIds = [setup.areaBaits.pp.id, setup.areaBaits.gpp.id];
      if (areaBaitIds.indexOf(getBaitItemId()) > -1)
        trap = setup[enviromentType][ppTrap][setup.isGoodGoTd ? gotd : normal];
      else trap = getNormalTrap(setup);
    }
    if (setup.isAutoMapping && mapInfo.isGpp && myItems.gpp > 2)
      trap[0] = setup.areaBaits.gpp.name;
    handleTrap(trap, quest, setup);
  }
  function getQuest() {
    const enviromentType = getEnvironmentType();
    switch (enviromentType) {
      case gwhAreas.hill:
        return user.quests.QuestCinnamonTreeGrove;
      case gwhAreas.workshop:
        return user.quests.QuestGolemWorkshop;
      case gwhAreas.fortress:
        return user.quests.QuestIceFortress;
      default:
        return null;
    }
  }
  function handleTrap(trap, quest, setup) {
    toggleFestiveSpirit(trap, quest, () => {
      if (trap) armAllTraps(...trap);
      if (setup.isAutoGolem)
        setTimeout(() => {
          checkGolem(setup);
        }, Math.floor(12000 + Math.random() * 3000));
    });
  }
  function getNormalTrap(setup) {
    return setup['normalTrap'][setup.isGoodGoTd ? gotd : normal];
  }
  // toggleFestiveSpirit([null,null,null,null,true,[true,false,false]], user.quests.QuestIceFortress);
  function toggleFestiveSpirit(trap, quest, callback) {
    const isEnable = trap ? trap[4] : false;
    const isCannonsEnable = trap ? trap[5] : [false, false, false];
    // user.quests.QuestCinnamonTreeGrove.is_fuel_enabled;
    const fsQty = MyUtils.parseQuantity(
      quest.items.festive_spirit_stat_item.quantity
    );
    // $('.headsUpDisplayWinterHuntRegionView__fuelButton', _container)
    // if (fsQty < 1) return;
    if (fsQty > 0 && (quest.is_fuel_enabled || false) != isEnable) {
      console.log('need to toggle FS');
      const uri = 'managers/ajax/events/winter_hunt_region.php';
      ajax(null, { action: 'toggle_fuel' }, uri).then(callback);
    } else callback();
    // cannon location checked in taggleCannons()
    setTimeout(() => {
      toggleCannons(isCannonsEnable);
    }, Math.floor(300 + Math.random() * 300));
  }
  // ajax(null, { action: 'toggle_fuel' }, 'managers/ajax/events/winter_hunt_region.php');
  // ajax(null, { action: 'toggle_maker', maker_type: 'head' }, 'managers/ajax/events/winter_hunt_golem_workshop.php');
  function ajax(element, params, uri) {
    console.log('GWH ajax');
    const callbackurl = 'https://www.mousehuntgame.com/';
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const lastReadJournalEntryId = parseInt(
        document
          .querySelector('div[data-entry-id]')
          .getAttribute('data-entry-id')
      );
      params['last_read_journal_entry_id'] = lastReadJournalEntryId;
      params['uh'] = user.unique_hash;
      $.ajax({
        data: params,
        dataType: 'json',
        type: 'post',
        url: callbackurl + uri, // 'managers/ajax/events/winter_hunt_region.php',
        success: function (data) {
          eventRegistry.doEvent('ajax_response', data);
          if (data.success) {
            resolve(data);
          } else {
            reject(data);
          }
        },
        error: function () {
          // eslint-disable-next-line new-cap
          const popup = new jsDialog();
          popup.setTemplate('error');
          popup.addToken('{*title*}', 'An error has occurred.');
          popup.addToken('{*content*}', 'An error occurred. Please try again!');
          popup.show();
          // target.removeClass('busy');
          // eslint-disable-next-line prefer-promise-reject-errors
          reject();
        }
      });
    });
  }
  // toggleCannons([true, false, true]);
  function toggleCannons(booleanArray, from, to) {
    const enviromentType = user.environment_type || '';
    console.log('currently in', enviromentType);
    if (enviromentType != 'winter_hunt_fortress') return;
    console.log(booleanArray, from, to);
    // const begin = from || 0;
    // const end = to || 2;
    let begin = from;
    let end = to;
    if (begin != 0 && !begin) begin = 0;
    if (end != 0 && !end) end = 2;
    if (begin > end) return;
    console.log('need to try toggle cannons', begin, end);
    let boolArray = booleanArray;
    if (!boolArray) boolArray = [false, false, false];
    toggleCannon(begin, boolArray[begin]);
    setTimeout(() => {
      toggleCannons(boolArray, ++begin, end);
    }, Math.floor(400 + Math.random() * 300));
  }
  function toggleCannon(index, isEnable) {
    const enviromentType = user.environment_type;
    console.log('currently in', enviromentType);
    if (enviromentType != 'winter_hunt_fortress') return;
    console.log('right location, try toggling');
    const quest = user.quests.QuestIceFortress;
    const cannonsState = [
      quest.cannons.snow_cannon.is_enabled || false,
      quest.cannons.cinnamon_cannon.is_enabled || false,
      quest.cannons.charm_cannon.is_enabled || false
    ];
    if (cannonsState[index] == isEnable) return;
    console.log('need to try toggle cannon', index);
    const cannonTypes = ['snow', 'cinnamon', 'charm'];
    const cannonType = cannonTypes[index];
    const uri = 'managers/ajax/events/winter_hunt_ice_fortress.php';
    const params = { action: 'toggle_cannon', cannon_type: cannonType };
    ajax(null, params, uri);
  }
  // toggleForges([false, false, false]);
  function toggleForges(booleanArray, from, to) {
    const enviromentType = user.environment_type || '';
    console.log('currently in', enviromentType);
    if (enviromentType != 'winter_hunt_workshop') return;
    console.log(booleanArray, from, to);
    // const begin = from || 0;
    // const end = to || 2;
    let begin = from;
    let end = to;
    if (begin != 0 && !begin) begin = 0;
    if (end != 0 && !end) end = 2;
    if (begin > end) return;
    console.log('need to try toggle forges', begin, end);
    let boolArray = booleanArray;
    if (!boolArray) boolArray = [false, false, false];
    toggleForge(begin, boolArray[begin]);
    setTimeout(() => {
      toggleForges(boolArray, ++begin, end);
    }, Math.floor(400 + Math.random() * 300));
  }
  function toggleForge(index, isEnable) {
    const enviromentType = user.environment_type;
    console.log('currently in', enviromentType);
    if (enviromentType != 'winter_hunt_workshop') return;
    console.log('right location, try toggling');
    const quest = user.quests.QuestGolemWorkshop;
    const forgesState = [
      quest.workshop.head.is_active || false,
      quest.workshop.torso.is_active || false,
      quest.workshop.limb.is_active || false
    ];
    if (forgesState[index] == isEnable) return;
    console.log('need to try toggle forge', index);
    const forgeTypes = ['head', 'torso', 'limb'];
    const forgeType = forgeTypes[index];
    const uri = 'managers/ajax/events/winter_hunt_golem_workshop.php';
    const params = { action: 'toggle_maker', maker_type: forgeType };
    ajax(null, params, uri);
  }
  function checkGolem(setup) {
    if (!setup.isAutoGolem) return;
    const quest = getQuest();
    console.log('Run checkGolem', quest);
    const golems = quest.golems;
    if (setup.isAutoUpgrade)
      for (let i = 0; i < setup.maxGolemsUse; i++) {
        const golem = golems[i];
        // 優先升級使用中(待 claim算使用中).
        // can_return不能用來判斷使用中,因為牽涉到有沒有 bell
        if (
          golem.can_upgrade &&
          (golem.hunts_remaining > 0 || golem.can_claim)
        ) {
          console.log('upgrade golem');
          upgradeGolem(golem.slot, () => {
            golem.can_upgrade = false;
            setTimeout(() => {
              checkGolem(setup);
            }, 1000);
          });
          // upgradeGolem(golem.slot);
          // golem.can_upgrade = false;
          // setTimeout(() => {
          //   checkGolem(setup);
          // }, Math.floor(setup.minGolemDelayTime + Math.random() * (setup.maxGolemDelayTime - setup.minGolemDelayTime)));
          return;
        }
      }
    for (let i = 0; i < setup.maxGolemsUse; i++) {
      const golem = golems[i];
      if (golem.can_claim) {
        console.log('claim golem');
        claimReward(golem.slot, () => {
          golem.can_claim = false;
          setTimeout(() => {
            checkGolem(setup);
          }, 1000);
        });
        // golem.can_claim = false;
        // setTimeout(() => {
        //   checkGolem(setup);
        // }, Math.floor(setup.minGolemDelayTime + Math.random() * (setup.maxGolemDelayTime - setup.minGolemDelayTime)));
        return;
      }
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < setup.maxGolemsUse; i++) {
      const golem = golems[i];
      if (golem.can_build) {
        console.log('build golem');
        buildGolem(golem.slot, setup.golemSetup.defaultTarget[i], () => {
          golem.can_build = false;
          setTimeout(() => {
            checkGolem(setup);
          }, 1000);
        });
        // golem.can_build = false;
        // setTimeout(() => {
        //   checkGolem(setup);
        // }, Math.floor(setup.minGolemDelayTime + Math.random() * (setup.maxGolemDelayTime - setup.minGolemDelayTime)));
        return;
      }
    }
    console.log('End checkGolem');
    /* const popup = new jsDialog();
    popup.addToken('{*title*}', 'End checkGolem.');
    popup.addToken('{*content*}', 'Check golem finished');
    popup.show(); */
  }
  // upgradeGolem(0);
  function upgradeGolem(slot, callback) {
    /* $('.headsUpDisplayWinterHuntRegionView__golemUpgradeButton')[0].click();
    $(
      '.greatWinterHuntGolemManagerUpgradeTabView__upgradeButton.greatWinterHuntDialogView__bigButton'
    ).click(); */
    $('.headsUpDisplayWinterHuntRegionView__golemUpgradeButton')[slot].click();
    // setTimeout(() => {
    //   document.getElementById('jsDialogClose').click();
    // }, 2000);
    const element = $(
      '.greatWinterHuntGolemManagerUpgradeTabView__upgradeButton.greatWinterHuntDialogView__bigButton'
    )[0];
    const params = {
      action: 'upgrade_golem',
      slot: slot
    };
    hg.views.GreatWinterHuntGolemManagerUpgradeTabView.ajax(
      element,
      params
    ).then(() => {
      const X = document.querySelector('#jsDialogClose');
      if (X) X.click();
      if (callback)
        setTimeout(() => {
          callback();
        }, 2000);
    });
  }

  // buildGolem(golem.slot, 'queso_river'); // floating_islands
  function buildGolem(slot, environmentType, callback) {
    const storageKey = 'scarfingDestinations';
    const scarfQty = getQuest().items.golden_scarf_stat_item.quantity;
    let scarfingDestination = null;
    let scarfingDestinations = null;
    if (scarfQty > 0) {
      scarfingDestinations = localStorage[storageKey];
      if (scarfingDestinations) {
        scarfingDestinations = JSON.parse(scarfingDestinations);
        if (scarfingDestinations.length > 0 && scarfingDestinations[0][1] != '')
          if (
            scarfingDestinations[0][0] == -1 ||
            scarfingDestinations[0][0] == slot
          )
            scarfingDestination = scarfingDestinations.shift(0)[1];
      }
    }
    const isScarf = scarfingDestination ? true : false;
    const isHat = false;
    const destination = scarfingDestination
      ? scarfingDestination
      : environmentType;
    $(
      '.headsUpDisplayWinterHuntRegionView__golemBuildButton.headsUpDisplayWinterHuntRegionView__golemActionButton'
    )[slot * 2 + 1].click();
    const element = $(
      '.greatWinterHuntDialogView__bigButton greatWinterHuntGolemManagerLaunchTabView__buildButton'
    )[0];
    // setTimeout(() => {
    const quest = getQuest();
    hg.views.GreatWinterHuntGolemManagerLaunchTabView.ajax(element, {
      action: 'build_golem',
      slot: slot,
      environment: destination,
      has_scarf: isScarf ? 1 : 0,
      has_hat: isHat ? 1 : 0,
      arms: quest.golems[slot].paperdoll.arms,
      head: quest.golems[slot].paperdoll.head,
      legs: quest.golems[slot].paperdoll.legs,
      torso: quest.golems[slot].paperdoll.torso
    }).then(() => {
      const X = document.querySelector('#jsDialogClose');
      if (X) X.click();
      if (callback)
        setTimeout(() => {
          callback();
        }, 2000);
    });
    // }, 2000);
    if (isScarf) {
      localStorage[storageKey] = JSON.stringify(scarfingDestinations);
      console.log(JSON.parse(localStorage[storageKey]));
    }
  }
  function travel(environmentType, milliseconds, callback) {
    // prettier-ignore
    // console.log('travel to:', environmentType, 'and reload after', milliseconds, 'seconds. callback', callback);
    if (user.environment_type == environmentType) return;
    const timeout = milliseconds ? milliseconds : 2000;
    hg.utils.User.travel(environmentType, () => {
      if (timeout >= 0) {
        window.setTimeout(function () {
          // console.log(callback);
          if (callback) callback();
          else window.location.href = 'https://www.mousehuntgame.com/';
        }, timeout);
      }
    });
  }
}

  }
  function ajax(element, params, uri) {
    console.log('GWH ajax');
    const callbackurl = 'https://www.mousehuntgame.com/';
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const lastReadJournalEntryId = parseInt(
        document
          .querySelector('div[data-entry-id]')
          .getAttribute('data-entry-id')
      );
      params['last_read_journal_entry_id'] = lastReadJournalEntryId;
      params['uh'] = user.unique_hash;
      $.ajax({
        data: params,
        dataType: 'json',
        type: 'post',
        url: callbackurl + uri, // 'managers/ajax/events/winter_hunt_region.php',
        success: function (data) {
          eventRegistry.doEvent('ajax_response', data);
          if (data.success) {
            resolve(data);
          } else {
            reject(data);
          }
        },
        error: function () {
          // eslint-disable-next-line new-cap
          const popup = new jsDialog();
          popup.setTemplate('error');
          popup.addToken('{*title*}', 'An error has occurred.');
          popup.addToken('{*content*}', 'An error occurred. Please try again!');
          popup.show();
          // target.removeClass('busy');
          // eslint-disable-next-line prefer-promise-reject-errors
          reject();
        }
      });
    });
  }
})();

(() => {
  const MapClasses = { treasuer: 'treasure', poster: 'poster', event: 'event' };
  const gwhAreas = {
    hill: 'winter_hunt_grove',
    workshop: 'winter_hunt_workshop',
    fortress: 'winter_hunt_fortress'
  };
  const isProd = true;
  const map = getMapByClass(MapClasses.event);
  hg.utils.TreasureMapUtil.getMapInfo(map.map_id, (data) => {
    const info = isProd ? data : mapInfo;
    console.log(parseMapInfo(info));
  });
  function parseMapInfo(mapInfo) {
    const rtn = {};
    const hunters = mapInfo.treasure_map.hunters;
    let completed = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < hunters.length; i++) {
      const hunter = hunters[i];
      const completedIds = hunter.completed_goal_ids.mouse;
      if (completedIds && completedIds.length > 0)
        completed = completed.concat(completedIds);
    }
    console.log(completed);
    const mouses = mapInfo.treasure_map.goals.mouse;
    for (let i = 0; i < mouses.length; i++) {
      const mouse = mouses[i];
      if (completed.indexOf(mouse.unique_id) > -1) continue;
      const type = mouse.type;
      if (['joy', 'glazed'].indexOf(type) > -1) rtn.isGpp = true;
      const areaIds = mouse.environment_ids;
      if (areaIds && areaIds.length === 1) {
        let area = null;
        switch (areaIds[0]) {
          case 68:
            area = gwhAreas.fortress;
            break;
          case 69:
            area = gwhAreas.hill;
            break;
          case 70:
            area = gwhAreas.workshop;
            break;
          default:
            break;
        }
        if (!rtn[area]) rtn[area] = [];
        rtn[area].push(type);
      } else {
        if (!rtn['all']) rtn['all'] = [];
        rtn['all'].push(type);
      }
    }
    return rtn;
  }
  function getMapByClass(mapClass) {
    const maps = user.quests.QuestRelicHunter.maps;
    for (let i = 0; i < maps.length; i++) {
      const element = maps[i];
      if (element.map_class == mapClass) {
        return element;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
})();

// MH & my-mh-aid functions
function getEnvironmentType() {
  const rtn = trimToEmpty(user.environment_type);
  // console.plog(`Current location environment_type: ${rtn}`);
  return rtn;
}
function trimToEmpty(str) {
  return trimToDefault(str);
}
function trimToDefault(str, deflt) {
  return str === 0 ? '0' : ((str || (deflt ? deflt : '')) + '').trim();
}
function logging(...any) {
  const debug = true;
  if (debug) {
    console.groupCollapsed(new Date(), ...any);
    console.log(Error().stack);
    console.groupEnd();
  }
}
function getStorageToObject(key, obj) {
  return obj;
}

}

console.plog = (...args) => {
  console.log(args);
};
function getMapByClass(mapClass) {
  const maps = user.quests.QuestRelicHunter.maps;
  for (let i = 0; i < maps.length; i++) {
    const element = maps[i];
    if (element.map_class == mapClass) {
      return element;
    }
  }
  return null;
}
/**
 * Get current bait item id.
 * @return {BigInt} current bait item id
 */
function getBaitItemId() {
  return MyUtils.parseQuantity(user.bait_item_id);
}

  console.log('arm following traps:', bait, weapon, base, charm);
}

// 這種大於多少跟小於多少才 arming的邏輯,其實只適用於絕對循序漸進變化的情形
// 例如離開後 cheese會歸零的情境才完全適用
// 像 mountain如果 cheese在大於跟小於中間時, travel過去就得手動初始 arming
// 畢竟沒有紀錄在大於跟小於中間時是正在往上走還是往下走
// 所以 MoPi windy/rainy才會額外增加 up/down
// 以這邊來說,正在往上走要 arm Gouda,正在往下走要 arm PP
// 也不能用給出預設值解,因為只要一 reload就變成預設值了=>原本用 Gouda往上走在大於跟小於中間,一 reload變成預設值 PP往下走了
// 更何況這邊要常常 travel來 travel去的
